by Artifact Studio Jewelry · SS27 Anklets

Sales image prompts

Nine prompts for each of the 40 anklets, in Etsy photo order. Slot 01 stays the approved hero; these fill slots 02 to 10. Use that anklet's own hero as the only reference image for all nine.

  • Model nano_banana_2
  • Resolution 2k
  • Aspect 1:1
  • Count 1 per prompt
  • Anklets 40
  • Prompts 360
  • Estimate about 720 credits

The nine frames

  1. 02
    On the ankle, studio

    The scale shot buyers look for first: one anklet, side view, charm against the ankle bone.

  2. 03
    Scale in the hand

    Charm in the palm with its size in mm and a pencil or fingernail comparison, so nobody is surprised by how small it is.

  3. 04
    Macro detail

    Kiln-fired enamel and gold rims up close: the quality proof for the price.

  4. 05
    Pool edge, hard sun

    The summer picture. Hard midday sun and crisp shadows give the contemporary editorial look.

  5. 06
    Year round, with flats

    Ballet flats and cropped linen trousers: anklets are now worn all year, not only at the beach.

  6. 07
    Postcard still life

    A colour-blocked still life with the family's own props (orange, sorbet, shells). This is the frame that makes the listing stand out.

  7. 08
    Gift moment

    Open linen box with a ribbon in the family colour. Gift buyers are a large share of jewelry sales on Etsy.

  8. 09
    Three gold colours

    Yellow, white and rose side by side: makes the 27 options (karat x colour x length) easy to see. Recolor visualization.

  9. 10
    In motion

    Walking up stone steps in sandals, chain swinging: movement makes a still listing feel alive.

Check before you keep a frame

  1. The charm matches its hero: same outline, same enamel colours, gold rims, nothing added.
  2. Exactly one anklet, on the left ankle only. Reject a second chain, toe rings or a charm on the other foot.
  3. Five toes on each foot, a believable ankle bone.
  4. The charm stays small: 6 to 10 mm, never bigger than the ankle bone.
  5. No text, numbers or logos anywhere, including the gift box.
  6. Lay the nine side by side before uploading: if two frames share the same framing, regenerate one.

If the hero's linen framing leaks into frames 04, 07 or 09, crop the reference to the charm and a few links of chain and generate again.

A01Wave Anklet Enamel · Luminous Blue · charm 8 mm · reference: hero A01-2
  1. 02

    On the ankle, studio

    This anklet with a flat wave-curl charm 8 mm, a single breaking wave whose crest curls over, filled with flat luminous cobalt blue (#1F4FD1) enamel inside a thin polished gold rim, one fine polished gold line following the curl. It is exactly one anklet, worn on the left ankle of a woman with medium olive skin; the charm rests on the outer side of the ankle just below the ankle bone; the right foot and ankle are bare; no second chain, no toe rings, no other jewelry; anatomically correct feet with five toes each, neatly trimmed natural toenails. The bare foot rests on a warm off-white linen throw, seen from the side at ankle height, cropped from mid-calf to toes, the charm in sharp focus and the background soft; the charm is only 8 mm across, about as wide as a pencil is thick, and the chain is a 1 mm thread of gold, small against the ankle bone. Shot on a Canon 50 mm lens at f/2, ISO 400, natural light only, fine 35 mm film grain, true colour, no heavy retouching. Natural skin texture visible. The anklet must be an exact copy of the anklet in the reference image: same charm outline, same colours and cell layout, same thin polished gold rims, same fine 1.0 mm cable chain, same gold jump ring; do not redesign it, do not add charms or stones; take only the anklet from the reference, never its background, camera angle or framing. Enamel is perfectly flat, glossy and flush inside thin polished gold rims, never domed, no gradient, no painted detail, no stones. No text, no numbers, no logo, no watermark, no ruler, no packaging print. Square 1:1.
